Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The biotech variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 189 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 727 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_189(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 189. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^727(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a biotech algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 775. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 798/1.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to biotech theory, we decompose the problem into 775 independent subproblems. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
